rk-arun78605:  WITH priority_shops AS (
         SELECT t.shop_code,
            t.priority_rank
           FROM ( VALUES ('SPN'::text,1), ('MSS'::text,2), ('LFS'::text,3), ('M03'::text,4), ('KAS'::text,5), ('MM1'::text,6), ('MM2'::text,7), ('FAR'::text,8), ('KS7'::text,9), ('WHL'::text,10), ('MM3'::text,11)) t(shop_code, priority_rank)
        ), item_wh_grn AS (
         SELECT TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(sup_shop_grn.item_code)) AS item_code,
            max(sup_shop_grn.wh_grn_date) AS wh_grn_date,
            (max(sup_shop_grn.wh_grn_date) + '30 days'::interval) AS wh_grn_plus_30
           FROM sup_shop_grn
          WHERE (sup_shop_grn.wh_grn_date IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(sup_shop_grn.item_code)))
        ), shop_expiry_latest AS (
         SELECT TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."ITEM_CODE")) AS item_code,
            TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."SHOP_CODE")) AS shop_code,
            max(shopexpiry."SHOP_EXPIRY_DATE") AS latest_expiry_date
           FROM shopexpiry
          GROUP BY (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."ITEM_CODE"))), (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."SHOP_CODE")))
        ), sources AS (
         SELECT im.itemcode AS item_code,
            im.itemname AS item_name,
            im.shopcode AS shop_code,
            im.shopstock AS stock,
            COALESCE(im.sales_30d_wh, (0)::numeric) AS sales_30d,
            im.shopgrn_dt AS last_grn_date,
                CASE
                    WHEN (im.shopgrn_dt IS NOT NULL) THEN (CURRENT_DATE - (im.shopgrn_dt)::date)
                    ELSE 0
                END AS grn_age,
            wh.wh_grn_date,
            im.groupp AS groups,
            im.subgroup AS sub_group,
            se.latest_expiry_date AS source_expiry_date,
                CASE
                    WHEN (se.latest_expiry_date IS NOT NULL) THEN ((se.latest_expiry_date)::date - CURRENT_DATE)
                    ELSE NULL::integer
                END AS source_expiry_days,
                CASE
                    WHEN (se.latest_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN NULL::text
                    WHEN (EXTRACT(year FROM (se.latest_expiry_date)::date) > (EXTRACT(year FROM CURRENT_DATE) + (2)::numeric)) THEN 'Check expiry date'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS expiry_check,
            im.importexport AS item_type,
            im.suppliername AS supplier_name
           FROM ((inventory_master im
             LEFT JOIN item_wh_grn wh ON ((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.itemcode)) = wh.item_code)))
             LEFT JOIN shop_expiry_latest se ON (((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.itemcode)) = se.item_code) AND (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.shopcode)) = se.shop_code))))
          WHERE ((NOT (im.shopcode IN ( SELECT priority_shops.shop_code
                   FROM priority_shops))) AND (im.shopstock > COALESCE(im.sales_30d_wh, (0)::numeric)) AND (im.shopstock > (0)::numeric))
        ), destinations AS (
         SELECT im.itemcode AS item_code,
            im.shopcode AS shop_code,
            im.shopstock AS stock,
            COALESCE(im.sales_30d_wh, (0)::numeric) AS sales_30d,
            im.shopgrn_dt AS last_grn_date,
                CASE
                    WHEN (im.shopgrn_dt IS NOT NULL) THEN (CURRENT_DATE - (im.shopgrn_dt)::date)
                    ELSE 0
                END AS grn_age,
            wh.wh_grn_date,
            wh.wh_grn_plus_30,
            ps.priority_rank
           FROM ((inventory_master im
             JOIN priority_shops ps ON ((im.shopcode = ps.shop_code)))
             LEFT JOIN item_wh_grn wh ON ((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.itemcode)) = wh.item_code)))
          WHERE (im.itemcode IS NOT NULL)
        ), dest_wh_grn_sales AS (
         SELECT d.item_code,
            d.shop_code,
            COALESCE(sum(s."QTY"), (0)::double precision) AS wh_grn_30d_sales
           FROM (destinations d
             LEFT JOIN sales_2024 s ON (((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."ITEM_CODE")) = d.item_code) AND (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."SHOP_CODE")) = d.shop_code) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date >= (d.wh_grn_date)::date) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date <= (d.wh_grn_plus_30)::date))))
          WHERE (d.wh_grn_date IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY d.item_code, d.shop_code
        UNION ALL
         SELECT d.item_code,
            d.shop_code,
            COALESCE(sum(s."QTY"), (0)::numeric) AS wh_grn_30d_sales
           FROM (destinations d
             LEFT JOIN sales_2025 s ON (((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."ITEM_CODE")) = d.item_code) AND (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."SHOP_CODE")) = d.shop_code) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date >= (d.wh_grn_date)::date) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date <= (d.wh_grn_plus_30)::date))))
          WHERE (d.wh_grn_date IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY d.item_code, d.shop_code
        ), dest_wh_grn_sales_agg AS (
         SELECT dest_wh_grn_sales.item_code,
            dest_wh_grn_sales.shop_code,
            sum(dest_wh_grn_sales.wh_grn_30d_sales) AS wh_grn_30d_sales
           FROM dest_wh_grn_sales
          GROUP BY dest_wh_grn_sales.item_code, dest_wh_grn_sales.shop_code
        ), dest_capacity AS (
         SELECT d.item_code,
            d.shop_code,
            d.stock,
            d.sales_30d,
            d.last_grn_date,
            d.grn_age,
            d.wh_grn_date,
            d.wh_grn_plus_30,
            d.priority_rank,
            COALESCE(wh.wh_grn_30d_sales, (0)::double precision) AS wh_grn_30d_sales,
            GREATEST(COALESCE(wh.wh_grn_30d_sales, (0)::double precision), (d.sales_30d)::double precision) AS cap
           FROM (destinations d
             LEFT JOIN dest_wh_grn_sales_agg wh ON (((d.item_code = wh.item_code) AND (d.shop_code = wh.shop_code))))
        ), all_combinations AS (
         SELECT s.item_code,
            s.item_name,
            s.shop_code AS source_shop,
            s.stock AS source_stock,
            s.sales_30d AS source_sales,
            s.last_grn_date AS source_last_grn,
            s.grn_age AS source_grn_age,
            s.wh_grn_date AS source_wh_grn_date,
            s.source_expiry_date,
            s.source_expiry_days,
            s.expiry_check,
            s.groups,
            s.sub_group,
            s.item_type,
            s.supplier_name,
            d.shop_code AS dest_shop,
            d.stock AS dest_stock,
            d.sales_30d AS dest_sales,
            d.last_grn_date AS dest_last_grn,
            d.grn_age AS dest_grn_age,
            d.wh_grn_date AS dest_wh_grn_date,
            d.wh_grn_plus_30 AS dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            d.wh_grn_30d_sales AS dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            d.cap AS dest_cap,
            d.priority_rank
           FROM (sources s
             JOIN dest_capacity d ON ((s.item_code = d.item_code)))
          WHERE (s.shop_code <> d.shop_code)
        ), uncapped_recs AS (
         SELECT all_combinations.item_code,
            all_combinations.item_name,
            all_combinations.source_shop,
            all_combinations.source_stock,
            all_combinations.source_sales,
            all_combinations.source_last_grn,
            all_combinations.source_grn_age,
            all_combinations.source_wh_grn_date,
            all_combinations.source_expiry_date,
            all_combinations.source_expiry_days,
            all_combinations.expiry_check,
            all_combinations.groups,
            all_combinations.sub_group,
            all_combinations.item_type,
            all_combinations.supplier_name,
            all_combinations.dest_shop,
            all_combinations.dest_stock,
            all_combinations.dest_sales,
            all_combinations.dest_last_grn,
            all_combinations.dest_grn_age,
            all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_date,
            all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            all_combinations.dest_cap,
            all_combinations.priority_rank,
            GREATEST((all_combinations.dest_cap - (all_combinations.dest_stock)::double precision), (0)::double precision) AS dest_gap,
            GREATEST((all_combinations.source_stock - all_combinations.source_sales), (0)::numeric) AS source_available,
            LEAST(GREATEST((all_combinations.dest_cap - (all_combinations.dest_stock)::double precision), (0)::double precision), (GREATEST((all_combinations.source_stock - all_combinations.source_sales), (0)::numeric))::double precision) AS uncapped_qty
           FROM all_combinations
          WHERE ((all_combinations.source_stock > all_combinations.source_sales) AND (NOT ((all_combinations.source_sales = (0)::numeric) AND (all_combinations.dest_sales = (0)::numeric) AND (all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales = (0)::double precision))) AND (NOT ((all_combinations.dest_stock > (30)::numeric) AND ((all_combinations.dest_stock)::double precision < LEAST((all_combinations.dest_sales)::double precision, all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales)))))
        ), capped_recs AS (
         SELECT uncapped_recs.item_code,
            uncapped_recs.item_name,
            uncapped_recs.source_shop,
            uncapped_recs.source_stock,
            uncapped_recs.source_sales,
            uncapped_recs.source_last_grn,
            uncapped_recs.source_grn_age,
            uncapped_recs.source_wh_grn_date,
            uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date,
            uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days,
            uncapped_recs.expiry_check,
            uncapped_recs.groups,
            uncapped_recs.sub_group,
            uncapped_recs.item_type,
            uncapped_recs.supplier_name,
            uncapped_recs.dest_shop,
            uncapped_recs.dest_stock,
            uncapped_recs.dest_sales,
            uncapped_recs.dest_last_grn,
            uncapped_recs.dest_grn_age,
            uncapped_recs.dest_wh_grn_date,
            uncapped_recs.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            uncapped_recs.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            uncapped_recs.dest_cap,
            uncapped_recs.priority_rank,
            uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty,
            uncapped_recs.source_available,
                CASE
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN 'OK'::text
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 0) THEN 'Expired'::text
                    WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 1) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days <= 29)) THEN 'Expiring'::text
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 30) THEN 'OK'::text
                    ELSE 'OK'::text
                END AS expiry_status,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 0) THEN (0)::double precision
                    WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 1) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days <= 29)) THEN (0)::double precision
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 30) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                    ELSE (0)::double precision
                END) OVER (PARTITION BY uncapped_recs.item_code, uncapped_recs.dest_shop ORDER BY uncapped_recs.priority_rank, uncapped_recs.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days, 999999), uncapped_recs.source_shop ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING) AS cumulative_before_this_row,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NOT NULL) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 30)) THEN (0)::double precision
                    ELSE LEAST(uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty, GREATEST((uncapped_recs.dest_cap - COALESCE(sum(
                    CASE
                        WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                        WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 0) THEN (0)::double precision
                        WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 1) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days <= 29)) THEN (0)::double precision
                        WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 30) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                        ELSE (0)::double precision
                    END) OVER (PARTITION BY uncapped_recs.item_code, uncapped_recs.dest_shop ORDER BY uncapped_recs.priority_rank, uncapped_recs.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days, 999999), uncapped_recs.source_shop ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING), (0)::double precision)), (0)::double precision))
                END AS recommended_qty
           FROM uncapped_recs
        ), source_allocation_check AS (
         SELECT capped_recs.item_code,
            capped_recs.item_name,
            capped_recs.source_shop,
            capped_recs.source_stock,
            capped_recs.source_sales,
            capped_recs.source_last_grn,
            capped_recs.source_grn_age,
            capped_recs.source_wh_grn_date,
            capped_recs.source_expiry_date,
            capped_recs.source_expiry_days,
            capped_recs.expiry_check,
            capped_recs.groups,
            capped_recs.sub_group,
            capped_recs.item_type,
            capped_recs.supplier_name,
            capped_recs.dest_shop,
            capped_recs.dest_stock,
            capped_recs.dest_sales,
            capped_recs.dest_last_grn,
            capped_recs.dest_grn_age,
            capped_recs.dest_wh_grn_date,
            capped_recs.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            capped_recs.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            capped_recs.dest_cap,
            capped_recs.priority_rank,
            capped_recs.uncapped_qty,
            capped_recs.source_available,
            capped_recs.expiry_status,
            capped_recs.cumulative_before_this_row,
            capped_recs.recommended_qty,
            sum(capped_recs.recommended_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY capped_recs.item_code, capped_recs.source_shop ORDER BY capped_recs.priority_rank, capped_recs.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(capped_recs.source_expiry_days, 999999), capped_recs.dest_shop) AS cumulative_source_allocated
           FROM capped_recs
        ), final_results AS (
         SELECT source_allocation_check.item_code,
            source_allocation_check.item_name,
            source_allocation_check.groups,
            source_allocation_check.sub_group,
            source_allocation_check.item_type,
            source_allocation_check.supplier_name,
            source_allocation_check.source_shop,
            source_allocation_check.source_stock,
            source_allocation_check.source_sales,
            source_allocation_check.source_last_grn,
            source_allocation_check.source_grn_age,
            source_allocation_check.source_wh_grn_date,
            source_allocation_check.source_expiry_date,
            source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days,
            source_allocation_check.expiry_check,
            source_allocation_check.expiry_status,
            source_allocation_check.dest_shop,
            source_allocation_check.dest_stock,
            source_allocation_check.dest_sales,
            source_allocation_check.dest_last_grn,
            source_allocation_check.dest_grn_age,
            source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_date,
            source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            source_allocation_check.dest_cap AS dest_sales_used,
            source_allocation_check.priority_rank,
            source_allocation_check.recommended_qty,
            sum(source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY source_allocation_check.item_code, source_allocation_check.dest_shop ORDER BY source_allocation_check.priority_rank, source_allocation_check.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days, 999999), source_allocation_check.source_shop) AS cumulative_qty,
            (source_allocation_check.dest_cap - COALESCE(sum(source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY source_allocation_check.item_code, source_allocation_check.dest_shop ORDER BY source_allocation_check.priority_rank, source_allocation_check.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days, 999999), source_allocation_check.source_shop ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING), (0)::double precision)) AS dest_remaining_cap_before,
            ((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) AS dest_updated_stock,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) > (0)::double precision) AND (GREATEST(source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales, (source_allocation_check.dest_sales)::double precision) > (0)::double precision)) THEN ceil(((((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) * (30.0)::double precision) / GREATEST(source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales, (source_allocation_check.dest_sales)::double precision)))
                    WHEN (((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) > (0)::double precision) THEN (999)::double precision
                    ELSE (0)::double precision
                END AS dest_final_stock_days,
                CASE
                    WHEN (source_allocation_check.expiry_status = 'Expired'::text) THEN 'Item expired - cannot transfer'::text
                    WHEN ((source_allocation_check.expiry_status = 'Expiring'::text) AND (source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days < 30)) THEN 'Expiring soon - cannot transfer'::text
                    WHEN (source_allocation_check.cumulative_source_allocated >= (source_allocation_check.source_available)::double precision) THEN 'Source stock fully allocated'::text
                    WHEN ((source_allocation_check.recommended_qty = (0)::double precision) AND (source_allocation_check.uncapped_qty > (0)::double precision)) THEN 'Cap reached'::text
                    WHEN ((source_allocation_check.recommended_qty > (0)::double precision) AND (source_allocation_check.recommended_qty < source_allocation_check.uncapped_qty)) THEN 'Partial allocation'::text
                    WHEN (source_allocation_check.recommended_qty > (0)::double precision) THEN 'Full allocation'::text
                    ELSE 'No transfer needed'::text
                END AS remark
           FROM source_allocation_check
        )
 SELECT item_code,
    item_name,

    dest_updated_stock,
    dest_final_stock_days,
    remark
   FROM final_results
  ORDER BY item_code, dest_shop, priority_rank, source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(source_expiry_days, 999999), source_shop;

## Summary

**Problem Found:** 🎯 Your dashboard isn't loading because the materialized view `mv_recommendations_complete` still references **`sales_2025_d`** (the truncated table) instead of **`sales_2025`** (your restored backup).

**Root Cause:** When you renamed the tables, the compiled materialized view kept the old table names hardcoded in its definition.

**Solution:** Recreate the materialized view. Run ONE of these commands in PowerShell:

**OPTION 1 (Recommended):**
```powershell

```

**What happens:** This will drop the old view and recreate it (takes 2-3 minutes), and the new view will correctly use `sales_2025` table.

After recreation completes, your dashboard will load normally:

CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_recommendations_complete AS
WITH priority_shops AS (
         SELECT t.shop_code,
            t.priority_rank
           FROM ( VALUES ('SPN'::text,1), ('MSS'::text,2), ('LFS'::text,3), ('M03'::text,4), ('KAS'::text,5), ('MM1'::text,6), ('MM2'::text,7), ('FAR'::text,8), ('KS7'::text,9), ('WHL'::text,10), ('MM3'::text,11)) t(shop_code, priority_rank)
        ), item_wh_grn AS (
         SELECT TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(sup_shop_grn.item_code)) AS item_code,
            max(sup_shop_grn.wh_grn_date) AS wh_grn_date,
            (max(sup_shop_grn.wh_grn_date) + '30 days'::interval) AS wh_grn_plus_30
           FROM sup_shop_grn
          WHERE (sup_shop_grn.wh_grn_date IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(sup_shop_grn.item_code)))
        ), shop_expiry_latest AS (
         SELECT TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."ITEM_CODE")) AS item_code,
            TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."SHOP_CODE")) AS shop_code,
            max(shopexpiry."SHOP_EXPIRY_DATE") AS latest_expiry_date
           FROM shopexpiry
          GROUP BY (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."ITEM_CODE"))), (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."SHOP_CODE")))
        ), sources AS (
         SELECT im.itemcode AS item_code,
            im.itemname AS item_name,
            im.shopcode AS shop_code,
            im.shopstock AS stock,
            COALESCE(im.sales_30d_wh, (0)::numeric) AS sales_30d,
            im.shopgrn_dt AS last_grn_date,
                CASE
                    WHEN (im.shopgrn_dt IS NOT NULL) THEN (CURRENT_DATE - (im.shopgrn_dt)::date)
                    ELSE 0
                END AS grn_age,
            wh.wh_grn_date,
            im.groupp AS groups,
            im.subgroup AS sub_group,
            se.latest_expiry_date AS source_expiry_date,
                CASE
                    WHEN (se.latest_expiry_date IS NOT NULL) THEN ((se.latest_expiry_date)::date - CURRENT_DATE)
                    ELSE NULL::integer
                END AS source_expiry_days,
                CASE
                    WHEN (se.latest_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN NULL::text
                    WHEN (EXTRACT(year FROM (se.latest_expiry_date)::date) > (EXTRACT(year FROM CURRENT_DATE) + (2)::numeric)) THEN 'Check expiry date'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS expiry_check,
            im.importexport AS item_type,
            im.suppliername AS supplier_name
           FROM ((inventory_master im
             LEFT JOIN item_wh_grn wh ON ((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.itemcode)) = wh.item_code)))
             LEFT JOIN shop_expiry_latest se ON (((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.itemcode)) = se.item_code) AND (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.shopcode)) = se.shop_code))))
          WHERE ((NOT (im.shopcode IN ( SELECT priority_shops.shop_code
                   FROM priority_shops))) AND (im.shopstock > COALESCE(im.sales_30d_wh, (0)::numeric)) AND (im.shopstock > (0)::numeric))
        ), destinations AS (
         SELECT im.itemcode AS item_code,
            im.shopcode AS shop_code,
            im.shopstock AS stock,
            COALESCE(im.sales_30d_wh, (0)::numeric) AS sales_30d,
            im.shopgrn_dt AS last_grn_date,
                CASE
                    WHEN (im.shopgrn_dt IS NOT NULL) THEN (CURRENT_DATE - (im.shopgrn_dt)::date)
                    ELSE 0
                END AS grn_age,
            wh.wh_grn_date,
            wh.wh_grn_plus_30,
            ps.priority_rank
           FROM ((inventory_master im
             JOIN priority_shops ps ON ((im.shopcode = ps.shop_code)))
             LEFT JOIN item_wh_grn wh ON ((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.itemcode)) = wh.item_code)))
          WHERE (im.itemcode IS NOT NULL)
        ), dest_wh_grn_sales AS (
         SELECT d.item_code,
            d.shop_code,
            COALESCE(sum(s."QTY"), (0)::double precision) AS wh_grn_30d_sales
           FROM (destinations d
             LEFT JOIN sales_2024 s ON (((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."ITEM_CODE")) = d.item_code) AND (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."SHOP_CODE")) = d.shop_code) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date >= (d.wh_grn_date)::date) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date <= (d.wh_grn_plus_30)::date))))
          WHERE (d.wh_grn_date IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY d.item_code, d.shop_code
        UNION ALL
         SELECT d.item_code,
            d.shop_code,
            COALESCE(sum(s."QTY"), (0)::numeric) AS wh_grn_30d_sales
           FROM (destinations d
             LEFT JOIN sales_2025 s ON (((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."ITEM_CODE")) = d.item_code) AND (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."SHOP_CODE")) = d.shop_code) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date >= (d.wh_grn_date)::date) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date <= (d.wh_grn_plus_30)::date))))
          WHERE (d.wh_grn_date IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY d.item_code, d.shop_code
        ), dest_wh_grn_sales_agg AS (
         SELECT dest_wh_grn_sales.item_code,
            dest_wh_grn_sales.shop_code,
            sum(dest_wh_grn_sales.wh_grn_30d_sales) AS wh_grn_30d_sales
           FROM dest_wh_grn_sales
          GROUP BY dest_wh_grn_sales.item_code, dest_wh_grn_sales.shop_code
        ), dest_capacity AS (
         SELECT d.item_code,
            d.shop_code,
            d.stock,
            d.sales_30d,
            d.last_grn_date,
            d.grn_age,
            d.wh_grn_date,
            d.wh_grn_plus_30,
            d.priority_rank,
            COALESCE(wh.wh_grn_30d_sales, (0)::double precision) AS wh_grn_30d_sales,
            GREATEST(COALESCE(wh.wh_grn_30d_sales, (0)::double precision), (d.sales_30d)::double precision) AS cap
           FROM (destinations d
             LEFT JOIN dest_wh_grn_sales_agg wh ON (((d.item_code = wh.item_code) AND (d.shop_code = wh.shop_code))))
        ), all_combinations AS (
         SELECT s.item_code,
            s.item_name,
            s.shop_code AS source_shop,
            s.stock AS source_stock,
            s.sales_30d AS source_sales,
            s.last_grn_date AS source_last_grn,
            s.grn_age AS source_grn_age,
            s.wh_grn_date AS source_wh_grn_date,
            s.source_expiry_date,
            s.source_expiry_days,
            s.expiry_check,
            s.groups,
            s.sub_group,
            s.item_type,
            s.supplier_name,
            d.shop_code AS dest_shop,
            d.stock AS dest_stock,
            d.sales_30d AS dest_sales,
            d.last_grn_date AS dest_last_grn,
            d.grn_age AS dest_grn_age,
            d.wh_grn_date AS dest_wh_grn_date,
            d.wh_grn_plus_30 AS dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            d.wh_grn_30d_sales AS dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            d.cap AS dest_cap,
            d.priority_rank
           FROM (sources s
             JOIN dest_capacity d ON ((s.item_code = d.item_code)))
          WHERE (s.shop_code <> d.shop_code)
        ), uncapped_recs AS (
         SELECT all_combinations.item_code,
            all_combinations.item_name,
            all_combinations.source_shop,
            all_combinations.source_stock,
            all_combinations.source_sales,
            all_combinations.source_last_grn,
            all_combinations.source_grn_age,
            all_combinations.source_wh_grn_date,
            all_combinations.source_expiry_date,
            all_combinations.source_expiry_days,
            all_combinations.expiry_check,
            all_combinations.groups,
            all_combinations.sub_group,
            all_combinations.item_type,
            all_combinations.supplier_name,
            all_combinations.dest_shop,
            all_combinations.dest_stock,
            all_combinations.dest_sales,
            all_combinations.dest_last_grn,
            all_combinations.dest_grn_age,
            all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_date,
            all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            all_combinations.dest_cap,
            all_combinations.priority_rank,
            GREATEST((all_combinations.dest_cap - (all_combinations.dest_stock)::double precision), (0)::double precision) AS dest_gap,
            GREATEST((all_combinations.source_stock - all_combinations.source_sales), (0)::numeric) AS source_available,
            LEAST(GREATEST((all_combinations.dest_cap - (all_combinations.dest_stock)::double precision), (0)::double precision), (GREATEST((all_combinations.source_stock - all_combinations.source_sales), (0)::numeric))::double precision) AS uncapped_qty
           FROM all_combinations
          WHERE ((all_combinations.source_stock > all_combinations.source_sales) AND (NOT ((all_combinations.source_sales = (0)::numeric) AND (all_combinations.dest_sales = (0)::numeric) AND (all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales = (0)::double precision))) AND (NOT ((all_combinations.dest_stock > (30)::numeric) AND ((all_combinations.dest_stock)::double precision < LEAST((all_combinations.dest_sales)::double precision, all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales)))))
        ), capped_recs AS (
         SELECT uncapped_recs.item_code,
            uncapped_recs.item_name,
            uncapped_recs.source_shop,
            uncapped_recs.source_stock,
            uncapped_recs.source_sales,
            uncapped_recs.source_last_grn,
            uncapped_recs.source_grn_age,
            uncapped_recs.source_wh_grn_date,
            uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date,
            uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days,
            uncapped_recs.expiry_check,
            uncapped_recs.groups,
            uncapped_recs.sub_group,
            uncapped_recs.item_type,
            uncapped_recs.supplier_name,
            uncapped_recs.dest_shop,
            uncapped_recs.dest_stock,
            uncapped_recs.dest_sales,
            uncapped_recs.dest_last_grn,
            uncapped_recs.dest_grn_age,
            uncapped_recs.dest_wh_grn_date,
            uncapped_recs.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            uncapped_recs.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            uncapped_recs.dest_cap,
            uncapped_recs.priority_rank,
            uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty,
            uncapped_recs.source_available,
                CASE
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN 'OK'::text
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 0) THEN 'Expired'::text
                    WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 1) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days <= 29)) THEN 'Expiring'::text
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 30) THEN 'OK'::text
                    ELSE 'OK'::text
                END AS expiry_status,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 0) THEN (0)::double precision
                    WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 1) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days <= 29)) THEN (0)::double precision
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 30) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                    ELSE (0)::double precision
                END) OVER (PARTITION BY uncapped_recs.item_code, uncapped_recs.dest_shop ORDER BY uncapped_recs.priority_rank, uncapped_recs.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days, 999999), uncapped_recs.source_shop ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING) AS cumulative_before_this_row,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NOT NULL) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 30)) THEN (0)::double precision
                    ELSE LEAST(uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty, GREATEST((uncapped_recs.dest_cap - COALESCE(sum(
                    CASE
                        WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                        WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 0) THEN (0)::double precision
                        WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 1) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days <= 29)) THEN (0)::double precision
                        WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 30) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                        ELSE (0)::double precision
                    END) OVER (PARTITION BY uncapped_recs.item_code, uncapped_recs.dest_shop ORDER BY uncapped_recs.priority_rank, uncapped_recs.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days, 999999), uncapped_recs.source_shop ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING), (0)::double precision)), (0)::double precision))
                END AS recommended_qty
           FROM uncapped_recs
        ), source_allocation_check AS (
         SELECT capped_recs.item_code,
            capped_recs.item_name,
            capped_recs.source_shop,
            capped_recs.source_stock,
            capped_recs.source_sales,
            capped_recs.source_last_grn,
            capped_recs.source_grn_age,
            capped_recs.source_wh_grn_date,
            capped_recs.source_expiry_date,
            capped_recs.source_expiry_days,
            capped_recs.expiry_check,
            capped_recs.groups,
            capped_recs.sub_group,
            capped_recs.item_type,
            capped_recs.supplier_name,
            capped_recs.dest_shop,
            capped_recs.dest_stock,
            capped_recs.dest_sales,
            capped_recs.dest_last_grn,
            capped_recs.dest_grn_age,
            capped_recs.dest_wh_grn_date,
            capped_recs.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            capped_recs.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            capped_recs.dest_cap,
            capped_recs.priority_rank,
            capped_recs.uncapped_qty,
            capped_recs.source_available,
            capped_recs.expiry_status,
            capped_recs.cumulative_before_this_row,
            capped_recs.recommended_qty,
            sum(capped_recs.recommended_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY capped_recs.item_code, capped_recs.source_shop ORDER BY capped_recs.priority_rank, capped_recs.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(capped_recs.source_expiry_days, 999999), capped_recs.dest_shop) AS cumulative_source_allocated
           FROM capped_recs
        ), final_results AS (
         SELECT source_allocation_check.item_code,
            source_allocation_check.item_name,
            source_allocation_check.groups,
            source_allocation_check.sub_group,
            source_allocation_check.item_type,
            source_allocation_check.supplier_name,
            source_allocation_check.source_shop,
            source_allocation_check.source_stock,
            source_allocation_check.source_sales,
            source_allocation_check.source_last_grn,
            source_allocation_check.source_grn_age,
            source_allocation_check.source_wh_grn_date,
            source_allocation_check.source_expiry_date,
            source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days,
            source_allocation_check.expiry_check,
            source_allocation_check.expiry_status,
            source_allocation_check.dest_shop,
            source_allocation_check.dest_stock,
            source_allocation_check.dest_sales,
            source_allocation_check.dest_last_grn,
            source_allocation_check.dest_grn_age,
            source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_date,
            source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            source_allocation_check.dest_cap AS dest_sales_used,
            source_allocation_check.priority_rank,
            source_allocation_check.recommended_qty,
            sum(source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY source_allocation_check.item_code, source_allocation_check.dest_shop ORDER BY source_allocation_check.priority_rank, source_allocation_check.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days, 999999), source_allocation_check.source_shop) AS cumulative_qty,
            (source_allocation_check.dest_cap - COALESCE(sum(source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY source_allocation_check.item_code, source_allocation_check.dest_shop ORDER BY source_allocation_check.priority_rank, source_allocation_check.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days, 999999), source_allocation_check.source_shop ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING), (0)::double precision)) AS dest_remaining_cap_before,
            ((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) AS dest_updated_stock,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) > (0)::double precision) AND (GREATEST(source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales, (source_allocation_check.dest_sales)::double precision) > (0)::double precision)) THEN ceil(((((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) * (30.0)::double precision) / GREATEST(source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales, (source_allocation_check.dest_sales)::double precision)))
                    WHEN (((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) > (0)::double precision) THEN (999)::double precision
                    ELSE (0)::double precision
                END AS dest_final_stock_days,
                CASE
                    WHEN (source_allocation_check.expiry_status = 'Expired'::text) THEN 'Item expired - cannot transfer'::text
                    WHEN ((source_allocation_check.expiry_status = 'Expiring'::text) AND (source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days < 30)) THEN 'Expiring soon - cannot transfer'::text
                    WHEN (source_allocation_check.cumulative_source_allocated >= (source_allocation_check.source_available)::double precision) THEN 'Source stock fully allocated'::text
                    WHEN ((source_allocation_check.recommended_qty = (0)::double precision) AND (source_allocation_check.uncapped_qty > (0)::double precision)) THEN 'Cap reached'::text
                    WHEN ((source_allocation_check.recommended_qty > (0)::double precision) AND (source_allocation_check.recommended_qty < source_allocation_check.uncapped_qty)) THEN 'Partial allocation'::text
                    WHEN (source_allocation_check.recommended_qty > (0)::double precision) THEN 'Full allocation'::text
                    ELSE 'No transfer needed'::text
                END AS remark
           FROM source_allocation_check
        )
 SELECT item_code,
    item_name,
    groups,
    sub_group,
    item_type,
    supplier_name,
    source_shop,
    source_stock,
    source_sales,
    source_last_grn,
    source_grn_age,
    source_wh_grn_date,
    source_expiry_date,
    source_expiry_days,
    expiry_check,
    expiry_status,
    dest_shop,
    dest_stock,
    dest_sales,
    dest_last_grn,
    dest_grn_age,
    dest_wh_grn_date,
    dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
    dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
    dest_sales_used,
    priority_rank,
    recommended_qty,
    cumulative_qty,
    dest_remaining_cap_before,
    dest_updated_stock,
    dest_final_stock_days,
    remark
   FROM final_results
  ORDER BY item_code, dest_shop, priority_rank, source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(source_expiry_days, 999999), source_shop;

GitHub Copilot: Created [](file:///d%3A/Dashboard%20Code/NO_WH/DS/VIEW_LOGIC_EXPLANATION_WITH_EXAMPLE.md)

Completed: *Execute view recreation* (2/3)

## Summary: Complete View Logic Explanation with Example

I've created a comprehensive guide: **VIEW_LOGIC_EXPLANATION_WITH_EXAMPLE.md** that explains the entire materialized view logic with a **real-world example** using Rice Bags.

### 🎯 Core Logic in 12 Steps:

1. **Priority Shops** - Define 11 priority destinations (SPN=1, MSS=2, ... MM3=11)
2. **WH GRN Dates** - Get warehouse received dates to track item age
3. **Expiry Tracking** - Get latest expiry dates per item+shop
4. **Source Identification** - Non-priority shops with excess stock (stock > sales)
5. **Destination Identification** - Priority shops only
6. **WH GRN +30d Sales** - Calculate post-arrival demand spike (first 30 days after WH received)
7. **Capacity Calculation** - Cap = MAX(wh_grn_30d_sales, regular_30d_sales) ← Uses higher demand
8. **Cross Join** - Create all source → destination combinations
9. **Uncapped Recommendations** - Calculate ideal transfer qty = MIN(dest_gap, source_available)
10. **Apply Capping** - Use window function to ensure cumulative allocation ≤ cap per destination
11. **Source Allocation Check** - Ensure total from source ≤ source_available
12. **Final Results** - Add remarks based on expiry, cap status, allocation status

### 📊 Real Example Summary:

**Item: RICE001**

4. **Cap enforcement** - Total to destination ≤ cap (never oversupply)
5. **Source limit** - Total from source ≤ available (never over-allocate)

rk-arun78605: # Stock Transfer Recommendation Logic - Complete Step-by-Step Guide

## Executive Summary

The recommendation system calculates how much stock to transfer from non-priority shops (sources) to priority shops (destinations) based on sales velocity and demand caps.

**For Item 157596 → SPN: Why only 27 units recommended when source has 80 stock?**

**Answer**: The recommendation is **CAPPED by SPN's demand**, not by the source shop's available stock. SPN can only absorb 27 units based on its sales capacity.

---

## The 7-Step Recommendation Process

### STEP 1: Identify Priority Destinations (11 Shops)

**Priority Shops (Can ONLY receive, never give):**
- SPN, MSS, LFS, M03, KAS, MM1, MM2, FAR, KS7, WHL, MM3

**Non-Priority Shops (Can give, never receive):**
- All other 78 shops (ACC, ACH, ADB, AF2, etc.)

**Business Rule**: Priority shops are high-volume stores that need continuous stock replenishment. Non-priority shops with slow-moving stock become sources.

---

### STEP 2: Calculate Destination Demand Cap

This is the **MOST CRITICAL** step that limits transfers.

#### Formula for Destination Cap:
```
Destination Cap = MAX(
    Destination's Last 30d Sales,
    Destination's WH GRN +30d Sales
)
```

**What is "WH GRN +30d Sales"?**
- WH GRN Date = When warehouse RECEIVED the item from supplier
- WH GRN +30d Sales = Sales from WH GRN Date to (WH GRN Date + 30 days)
- Captures immediate demand spike after new stock arrives

#### Example for Item 157596 → SPN:

| Metric | Value | Calculation |
|--------|-------|-------------|
| SPN Last 30d Sales | 25 units | Sales from Nov 8 to Dec 7 |
| WH GRN Date | Nov 15, 2025 | Warehouse received item |
| WH GRN +30 Date | Dec 15, 2025 | 30 days after WH GRN |
| WH GRN +30d Sales | 27 units | Sales from Nov 15 to Dec 15 |
| **SPN Demand Cap** | **27 units** | MAX(25, 27) = 27 |

**Why use WH GRN +30d Sales?**
- New stock arrival often triggers sales surge (promotions, restocking effect)
- Captures true demand potential better than historical 30d sales
- Prevents under-allocating to shops with recent high demand

---

### STEP 3: Identify Source Shops with Excess Stock

A shop becomes a SOURCE if:

```
Source Shop Stock > Source Shop's Last 30d Sales
```

**Logic**: If shop's stock exceeds its sales velocity, it has excess that can be transferred.

#### Example for Item 157596:

| Shop | Stock | Last 30d Sales | Excess? | Available to Transfer |
|------|-------|----------------|---------|----------------------|
| ACC  | 80    | 15             | ✅ Yes  | 80 - 15 = 65 units   |
| HOE  | 45    | 12             | ✅ Yes  | 45 - 12 = 33 units   |
| KS5  | 30    | 8              | ✅ Yes  | 30 - 8 = 22 units    |
| ADB  | 20    | 25             | ❌ No   | Stock < Sales (keep) |

**Source Selection Rules:**
1. Must NOT be a priority shop (priority shops never give)
2. Must have stock > 0
3. Must have stock > their own 30d sales (excess stock)

---

### STEP 4: Calculate Initial Recommended Quantity

For each Source → Destination pair:

```
Initial Recommended Qty = MIN(
    Source's Available Stock,
    Destination's Demand Cap
)
```

#### Example for Item 157596:

| Source Shop | Available Stock | SPN Cap | Initial Recommendation |
|-------------|----------------|---------|------------------------|
| ACC         | 65 units       | 27      | MIN(65, 27) = 27       |
| HOE         | 33 units       | 27      | MIN(33, 27) = 27       |
| KS5         | 22 units       | 27      | MIN(22, 27) = 22       |

**Total if uncapped: 27 + 27 + 22 = 76 units**

But SPN can only absorb 27 units total!

---

### STEP 5: Apply Destination Cap with Priority Allocation

**CRITICAL RULE**: Total allocations to ONE destination from ALL sources cannot exceed destination's cap.

#### Allocation Priority (in order):
1. **Oldest GRN Date** (FIFO - First In, First Out)
2. **Largest Quantity** (bigger transfers first)
3. **Priority Shops First** (if source were priority)
4. **Alphabetical** (tiebreaker)

#### Example for Item 157596 → SPN (Cap = 27):

| Source | Available | GRN Date | GRN Age (days) | Initial Rec | Priority Rank |
|--------|-----------|----------|----------------|-------------|---------------|
| ACC    | 65        | Oct 15   | 54 days        | 27          | 1st (oldest)  |
| HOE    | 33        | Nov 20   | 18 days        | 27          | 2nd           |
| KS5    | 22        | Nov 25   | 13 days        | 22          | 3rd           |

**Allocation Walk-Through:**

```
Running Total = 0
Cap = 27

1. Process ACC (oldest):
   - Requested: 27
   - Remaining Cap: 27 - 0 = 27
   - Allocated: MIN(27, 27) = 27 ✅
   - Running Total: 0 + 27 = 27
   - Status: Cap REACHED

2. Process HOE:
   - Requested: 27
   - Remaining Cap: 27 - 27 = 0
   - Allocated: MIN(27, 0) = 0 ❌ CAPPED
   - Remark: "Destination cap reached"

3. Process KS5:
   - Requested: 22
   - Remaining Cap: 27 - 27 = 0
   - Allocated: MIN(22, 0) = 0 ❌ CAPPED
   - Remark: "Destination cap reached"
```

**Final Result for SPN:**
- ACC → SPN: 27 units ✅
- HOE → SPN: 0 units (capped)
- KS5 → SPN: 0 units (capped)

---

### STEP 6: Apply Same Logic to Other Destinations

The system repeats Steps 4-5 for ALL priority destinations:

#### Example for Item 157596 → Multiple Destinations:

| Source | Stock | SPN Cap | LFS Cap | FAR Cap | Total Possible |
|--------|-------|---------|---------|---------|----------------|
| ACC    | 65    | 27      | 15      | 12      | 54             |
| HOE    | 33    | 0       | 15      | 10      | 25             |
| KS5    | 22    | 0       | 0       | 12      | 12             |

**Why HOE gets 0 to SPN but 15 to LFS?**
- SPN's cap was already filled by ACC (older stock)
- LFS has its own separate cap of 15 units
- HOE can still transfer to LFS because LFS's cap is independent

---

### STEP 7: Validate Source Shop Constraints

**Final Check**: Ensure total transfers FROM each source don't exceed source's available stock.

#### Example for Source Shop ACC:

| Destination | Allocated | Source Stock Check |
|-------------|-----------|-------------------|
| SPN         | 27        | Running: 27/65    |
| LFS         | 15        | Running: 42/65    |
| FAR         | 12        | Running: 54/65    |
| MM2         | 8         | Running: 62/65    |
| **Total**   | **62**    | ✅ 62 ≤ 65 (OK)   |

If total exceeded 65, the last allocations would be reduced.

---

## Key Business Logic Points

### Why Cap Matters More Than Source Stock

**Scenario**: Source has 1000 units, but destination only sold 5 in last 30 days.

**Question**: Should we transfer 1000 units?

**Answer**: NO! Transfer only 5 units.

**Reason**: 
- Destination can only sell 5 units per 30 days
- Transferring 1000 would create inventory pile-up
- Blocks destination's cash flow
- Risk of expiry/damage

### Why Use WH GRN +30d Sales?

**Scenario**: New product launch on Nov 15.

| Period | Sales |
|--------|-------|
| Oct 15 - Nov 14 (before launch) | 5 units |
| Nov 15 - Dec 14 (after launch) | 50 units |
| Last 30d Sales (Nov 8 - Dec 7) | 45 units |
| WH GRN +30d Sales (Nov 15 - Dec 15) | 50 units |

**Recommended Cap**: MAX(45, 50) = 50 units

**Benefit**: Captures the full post-launch demand spike.

### Why FIFO (Oldest First)?

**Inventory Management Best Practice**:
- Prevents stock from aging
- Reduces expiry risk
- Balances inventory across network
- Shop with oldest stock gets priority to offload

---

## Real-World Example Walkthrough

### Item 157596 Complete Scenario

**Background:**
- Item: Consumer Electronics (Fast-moving)
- WH GRN Date: Nov 15, 2025
- Current Date: Dec 8, 2025

**Source Shops (Non-Priority with Excess):**

| Shop | Stock | GRN Date | GRN Age | Last 30d Sales | Excess Stock |
|------|-------|----------|---------|----------------|--------------|
| ACC  | 80    | Oct 15   | 54 days | 15             | 65           |
| HOE  | 45    | Nov 20   | 18 days | 12             | 33           |
| KS5  | 30    | Nov 25   | 13 days | 8              | 22           |
| ADB  | 20    | Dec 1    | 7 days  | 5              | 15           |

**Priority Destinations with Caps:**

| Shop | Last 30d Sales | WH GRN +30d Sales | **Cap** |
|------|----------------|-------------------|---------|
| SPN  | 25             | 27                | **27**  |
| LFS  | 12             | 15                | **15**  |
| FAR  | 10             | 12                | **12**  |
| MM2  | 8              | 8                 | **8**   |

**Allocation Matrix (After Capping):**

| Source | SPN (27) | LFS (15) | FAR (12) | MM2 (8) | Total Used | Stock Left |
|--------|----------|----------|----------|---------|------------|------------|
| ACC    | **27**   | 15       | 12       | 8       | 62         | 18         |
| HOE    | 0        | 0        | 0        | 0       | 0          | 33         |
| KS5    | 0        | 0        | 0        | 0       | 0          | 22         |
| ADB    | 0        | 0        | 0        | 0       | 0          | 15         |

**Why ACC gets everything?**
- ACC has the OLDEST stock (Oct 15, 54 days old)
- System prioritizes oldest stock first (FIFO)
- ACC's 65 available units can cover all demands (27+15+12+8 = 62)
- HOE, KS5, ADB are "backup" sources if ACC stock was insufficient

## Stakeholder Communication Points

### For Finance/Management:

**Q: Why not transfer all excess stock immediately?**

**A**: Transferring is based on DEMAND, not just SUPPLY:
- Prevents inventory pile-up at destinations
- Maintains healthy inventory turnover
- Reduces carrying costs
- Minimizes expiry risk

### For Store Managers:

**Q: Why is my shop (HOE) not transferring when I have 33 units excess?**

**A**: Priority allocation system:
1. Other shops have older stock (need to clear first)
2. Priority destinations' caps are already filled
3. Your stock becomes available for next allocation cycle
4. Ensures fair FIFO distribution across network

### For Warehouse Operations:

**Q: How to prioritize picking/packing?**

**A**: Recommendations are pre-sorted by:
1. Source GRN Age (oldest first) - **Most Important**
2. Destination priority rank
3. Quantity size (bigger transfers first)

---

## Common Questions & Answers

### Q1: Can total recommendations exceed source stock?
**A**: NO. Final validation ensures: 
```
SUM(all transfers FROM source) ≤ Source Available Stock
```

### Q2: Can total recommendations exceed destination cap?
**A**: NO. Capping logic ensures:
```
SUM(all transfers TO destination) ≤ Destination Cap
```

### Q3: Why does the same item have different recommendations to different shops?
**A**: Each destination has its own independent cap based on ITS sales velocity.

### Q4: Can a priority shop be a source?
**A**: NO. Priority shops = High-volume stores that ONLY receive, never give.

### Q5: What if destination has NO sales in last 30 days?
**A**: Cap = 0, therefore NO transfers recommended (prevents dead stock accumulation).

### Q6: How often should recommendations be refreshed?
**A**: Daily or weekly, as sales patterns and stock levels change.

---

## Mathematical Formulas Summary

```
1. Source Available Stock = Source Stock - Source Last 30d Sales

2. Destination Cap = MAX(
     Destination Last 30d Sales,
     Destination WH GRN +30d Sales
   )

3. Initial Recommendation = MIN(
     Source Available Stock,
     Destination Cap
   )

4. Final Allocation (for each destination):
   FOR each source (ordered by GRN age DESC):
     remaining_cap = Destination Cap - allocated_so_far
     allocated = MIN(Initial Recommendation, remaining_cap)
     allocated_so_far += allocated

5. Source Validation:
   SUM(all allocations from source) ≤ Source Available Stock
```

---

---

## Conclusion: Why Item 157596 Shows 27 to SPN

**Answer in 3 Points:**

1. **Cap-Based, Not Stock-Based**: 
   - Recommendation = 27 because SPN's demand cap = 27
   - NOT because source (ACC) only has 27 (ACC actually has 80)

2. **Demand-Driven Logic**:
   - SPN sold 27 units in the high-demand period (WH GRN +30d)
   - Transferring more would exceed SPN's sales velocity
   - Prevents inventory accumulation

3. **FIFO Priority**:
   - ACC has oldest stock (54 days)
   - Gets first allocation rights to all destinations
   - Other sources (HOE, KS5) are backups

**Bottom Line**: The system recommends based on **destination's ability to sell**, not source's ability to give. This ensures healthy inventory turnover and prevents dead stock.

- **AFL**: 13 stock (< 30) ❌ EXCLUDED
- **KCS**: 4 stock (< 30) ❌ EXCLUDED  
- **M04**: 80 stock (> 30) ✅ INCLUDED
- **MAS**: 19 stock (< 30) ❌ EXCLUDED
- **SD2**: 107 stock (> 30) ✅ INCLUDED

```
Source Available = Stock - 30 (but can go negative/zero)
If Source Available > 0, then transfer that amount

```

So a shop with 35 units can transfer 5 units (35-30=5).

Made changes.

rk-arun78605: THIS IS THE RECOMMENDATION COMPLETE CODE THAT I WAS HAVING 


 WITH priority_shops AS (
         SELECT t.shop_code,
            t.priority_rank
           FROM ( VALUES ('SPN'::text,1), ('MSS'::text,2), ('LFS'::text,3), ('M03'::text,4), ('KAS'::text,5), ('MM1'::text,6), ('MM2'::text,7), ('FAR'::text,8), ('KS7'::text,9), ('WHL'::text,10), ('MM3'::text,11)) t(shop_code, priority_rank)
        ), item_wh_grn AS (
         SELECT TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(sup_shop_grn.item_code)) AS item_code,
            max(sup_shop_grn.wh_grn_date) AS wh_grn_date,
            (max(sup_shop_grn.wh_grn_date) + '30 days'::interval) AS wh_grn_plus_30
           FROM sup_shop_grn
          WHERE (sup_shop_grn.wh_grn_date IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(sup_shop_grn.item_code)))
        ), shop_expiry_latest AS (
         SELECT TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."ITEM_CODE")) AS item_code,
            TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."SHOP_CODE")) AS shop_code,
            max(shopexpiry."SHOP_EXPIRY_DATE") AS latest_expiry_date
           FROM shopexpiry
          GROUP BY (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."ITEM_CODE"))), (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(shopexpiry."SHOP_CODE")))
        ), sources AS (
         SELECT im.itemcode AS item_code,
            im.itemname AS item_name,
            im.shopcode AS shop_code,
            im.shopstock AS stock,
            COALESCE(im.sales_30d_wh, (0)::numeric) AS sales_30d,
            im.shopgrn_dt AS last_grn_date,
                CASE
                    WHEN (im.shopgrn_dt IS NOT NULL) THEN (CURRENT_DATE - (im.shopgrn_dt)::date)
                    ELSE 0
                END AS grn_age,
            wh.wh_grn_date,
            im.groupp AS groups,
            im.subgroup AS sub_group,
            se.latest_expiry_date AS source_expiry_date,
                CASE
                    WHEN (se.latest_expiry_date IS NOT NULL) THEN ((se.latest_expiry_date)::date - CURRENT_DATE)
                    ELSE NULL::integer
                END AS source_expiry_days,
                CASE
                    WHEN (se.latest_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN NULL::text
                    WHEN (EXTRACT(year FROM (se.latest_expiry_date)::date) > (EXTRACT(year FROM CURRENT_DATE) + (2)::numeric)) THEN 'Check expiry date'::text
                    ELSE NULL::text
                END AS expiry_check,
            im.importexport AS item_type,
            im.suppliername AS supplier_name
           FROM ((inventory_master im
             LEFT JOIN item_wh_grn wh ON ((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.itemcode)) = wh.item_code)))
             LEFT JOIN shop_expiry_latest se ON (((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.itemcode)) = se.item_code) AND (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.shopcode)) = se.shop_code))))
          WHERE ((NOT (im.shopcode IN ( SELECT priority_shops.shop_code
                   FROM priority_shops))) AND (im.shopstock > COALESCE(im.sales_30d_wh, (0)::numeric)) AND (im.shopstock > (0)::numeric))
        ), destinations AS (
         SELECT im.itemcode AS item_code,
            im.shopcode AS shop_code,
            im.shopstock AS stock,
            COALESCE(im.sales_30d_wh, (0)::numeric) AS sales_30d,
            im.shopgrn_dt AS last_grn_date,
                CASE
                    WHEN (im.shopgrn_dt IS NOT NULL) THEN (CURRENT_DATE - (im.shopgrn_dt)::date)
                    ELSE 0
                END AS grn_age,
            wh.wh_grn_date,
            wh.wh_grn_plus_30,
            ps.priority_rank
           FROM ((inventory_master im
             JOIN priority_shops ps ON ((im.shopcode = ps.shop_code)))
             LEFT JOIN item_wh_grn wh ON ((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(im.itemcode)) = wh.item_code)))
          WHERE (im.itemcode IS NOT NULL)
        ), dest_wh_grn_sales AS (
         SELECT d.item_code,
            d.shop_code,
            COALESCE(sum(s."QTY"), (0)::double precision) AS wh_grn_30d_sales
           FROM (destinations d
             LEFT JOIN sales_2024 s ON (((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."ITEM_CODE")) = d.item_code) AND (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."SHOP_CODE")) = d.shop_code) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date >= (d.wh_grn_date)::date) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date <= (d.wh_grn_plus_30)::date))))
          WHERE (d.wh_grn_date IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY d.item_code, d.shop_code
        UNION ALL
         SELECT d.item_code,
            d.shop_code,
            COALESCE(sum(s."QTY"), (0)::numeric) AS wh_grn_30d_sales
           FROM (destinations d
             LEFT JOIN sales_2025 s ON (((TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."ITEM_CODE")) = d.item_code) AND (TRIM(BOTH FROM upper(s."SHOP_CODE")) = d.shop_code) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date >= (d.wh_grn_date)::date) AND ((s."DATE_INVOICE")::date <= (d.wh_grn_plus_30)::date))))
          WHERE (d.wh_grn_date IS NOT NULL)
          GROUP BY d.item_code, d.shop_code
        ), dest_wh_grn_sales_agg AS (
         SELECT dest_wh_grn_sales.item_code,
            dest_wh_grn_sales.shop_code,
            sum(dest_wh_grn_sales.wh_grn_30d_sales) AS wh_grn_30d_sales
           FROM dest_wh_grn_sales
          GROUP BY dest_wh_grn_sales.item_code, dest_wh_grn_sales.shop_code
        ), dest_capacity AS (
         SELECT d.item_code,
            d.shop_code,
            d.stock,
            d.sales_30d,
            d.last_grn_date,
            d.grn_age,
            d.wh_grn_date,
            d.wh_grn_plus_30,
            d.priority_rank,
            COALESCE(wh.wh_grn_30d_sales, (0)::double precision) AS wh_grn_30d_sales,
            GREATEST(COALESCE(wh.wh_grn_30d_sales, (0)::double precision), (d.sales_30d)::double precision) AS cap
           FROM (destinations d
             LEFT JOIN dest_wh_grn_sales_agg wh ON (((d.item_code = wh.item_code) AND (d.shop_code = wh.shop_code))))
        ), all_combinations AS (
         SELECT s.item_code,
            s.item_name,
            s.shop_code AS source_shop,
            s.stock AS source_stock,
            s.sales_30d AS source_sales,
            s.last_grn_date AS source_last_grn,
            s.grn_age AS source_grn_age,
            s.wh_grn_date AS source_wh_grn_date,
            s.source_expiry_date,
            s.source_expiry_days,
            s.expiry_check,
            s.groups,
            s.sub_group,
            s.item_type,
            s.supplier_name,
            d.shop_code AS dest_shop,
            d.stock AS dest_stock,
            d.sales_30d AS dest_sales,
            d.last_grn_date AS dest_last_grn,
            d.grn_age AS dest_grn_age,
            d.wh_grn_date AS dest_wh_grn_date,
            d.wh_grn_plus_30 AS dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            d.wh_grn_30d_sales AS dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            d.cap AS dest_cap,
            d.priority_rank
           FROM (sources s
             JOIN dest_capacity d ON ((s.item_code = d.item_code)))
          WHERE (s.shop_code <> d.shop_code)
        ), uncapped_recs AS (
         SELECT all_combinations.item_code,
            all_combinations.item_name,
            all_combinations.source_shop,
            all_combinations.source_stock,
            all_combinations.source_sales,
            all_combinations.source_last_grn,
            all_combinations.source_grn_age,
            all_combinations.source_wh_grn_date,
            all_combinations.source_expiry_date,
            all_combinations.source_expiry_days,
            all_combinations.expiry_check,
            all_combinations.groups,
            all_combinations.sub_group,
            all_combinations.item_type,
            all_combinations.supplier_name,
            all_combinations.dest_shop,
            all_combinations.dest_stock,
            all_combinations.dest_sales,
            all_combinations.dest_last_grn,
            all_combinations.dest_grn_age,
            all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_date,
            all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            all_combinations.dest_cap,
            all_combinations.priority_rank,
            GREATEST((all_combinations.dest_cap - (all_combinations.dest_stock)::double precision), (0)::double precision) AS dest_gap,
            GREATEST((all_combinations.source_stock - all_combinations.source_sales), (0)::numeric) AS source_available,
            LEAST(GREATEST((all_combinations.dest_cap - (all_combinations.dest_stock)::double precision), (0)::double precision), (GREATEST((all_combinations.source_stock - all_combinations.source_sales), (0)::numeric))::double precision) AS uncapped_qty
           FROM all_combinations
          WHERE ((all_combinations.source_stock > all_combinations.source_sales) AND (NOT ((all_combinations.source_sales = (0)::numeric) AND (all_combinations.dest_sales = (0)::numeric) AND (all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales = (0)::double precision))) AND (NOT ((all_combinations.dest_stock > (30)::numeric) AND ((all_combinations.dest_stock)::double precision < LEAST((all_combinations.dest_sales)::double precision, all_combinations.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales)))))
        ), capped_recs AS (
         SELECT uncapped_recs.item_code,
            uncapped_recs.item_name,
            uncapped_recs.source_shop,
            uncapped_recs.source_stock,
            uncapped_recs.source_sales,
            uncapped_recs.source_last_grn,
            uncapped_recs.source_grn_age,
            uncapped_recs.source_wh_grn_date,
            uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date,
            uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days,
            uncapped_recs.expiry_check,
            uncapped_recs.groups,
            uncapped_recs.sub_group,
            uncapped_recs.item_type,
            uncapped_recs.supplier_name,
            uncapped_recs.dest_shop,
            uncapped_recs.dest_stock,
            uncapped_recs.dest_sales,
            uncapped_recs.dest_last_grn,
            uncapped_recs.dest_grn_age,
            uncapped_recs.dest_wh_grn_date,
            uncapped_recs.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            uncapped_recs.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            uncapped_recs.dest_cap,
            uncapped_recs.priority_rank,
            uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty,
            uncapped_recs.source_available,
                CASE
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN 'OK'::text
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 0) THEN 'Expired'::text
                    WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 1) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days <= 29)) THEN 'Expiring'::text
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 30) THEN 'OK'::text
                    ELSE 'OK'::text
                END AS expiry_status,
            sum(
                CASE
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 0) THEN (0)::double precision
                    WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 1) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days <= 29)) THEN (0)::double precision
                    WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 30) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                    ELSE (0)::double precision
                END) OVER (PARTITION BY uncapped_recs.item_code, uncapped_recs.dest_shop ORDER BY uncapped_recs.priority_rank, uncapped_recs.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days, 999999), uncapped_recs.source_shop ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING) AS cumulative_before_this_row,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NOT NULL) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 30)) THEN (0)::double precision
                    ELSE LEAST(uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty, GREATEST((uncapped_recs.dest_cap - COALESCE(sum(
                    CASE
                        WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_date IS NULL) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                        WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days < 0) THEN (0)::double precision
                        WHEN ((uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 1) AND (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days <= 29)) THEN (0)::double precision
                        WHEN (uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days >= 30) THEN uncapped_recs.uncapped_qty
                        ELSE (0)::double precision
                    END) OVER (PARTITION BY uncapped_recs.item_code, uncapped_recs.dest_shop ORDER BY uncapped_recs.priority_rank, uncapped_recs.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(uncapped_recs.source_expiry_days, 999999), uncapped_recs.source_shop ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING), (0)::double precision)), (0)::double precision))
                END AS recommended_qty
           FROM uncapped_recs
        ), source_allocation_check AS (
         SELECT capped_recs.item_code,
            capped_recs.item_name,
            capped_recs.source_shop,
            capped_recs.source_stock,
            capped_recs.source_sales,
            capped_recs.source_last_grn,
            capped_recs.source_grn_age,
            capped_recs.source_wh_grn_date,
            capped_recs.source_expiry_date,
            capped_recs.source_expiry_days,
            capped_recs.expiry_check,
            capped_recs.groups,
            capped_recs.sub_group,
            capped_recs.item_type,
            capped_recs.supplier_name,
            capped_recs.dest_shop,
            capped_recs.dest_stock,
            capped_recs.dest_sales,
            capped_recs.dest_last_grn,
            capped_recs.dest_grn_age,
            capped_recs.dest_wh_grn_date,
            capped_recs.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            capped_recs.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            capped_recs.dest_cap,
            capped_recs.priority_rank,
            capped_recs.uncapped_qty,
            capped_recs.source_available,
            capped_recs.expiry_status,
            capped_recs.cumulative_before_this_row,
            capped_recs.recommended_qty,
            sum(capped_recs.recommended_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY capped_recs.item_code, capped_recs.source_shop ORDER BY capped_recs.priority_rank, capped_recs.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(capped_recs.source_expiry_days, 999999), capped_recs.dest_shop) AS cumulative_source_allocated
           FROM capped_recs
        ), final_results AS (
         SELECT source_allocation_check.item_code,
            source_allocation_check.item_name,
            source_allocation_check.groups,
            source_allocation_check.sub_group,
            source_allocation_check.item_type,
            source_allocation_check.supplier_name,
            source_allocation_check.source_shop,
            source_allocation_check.source_stock,
            source_allocation_check.source_sales,
            source_allocation_check.source_last_grn,
            source_allocation_check.source_grn_age,
            source_allocation_check.source_wh_grn_date,
            source_allocation_check.source_expiry_date,
            source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days,
            source_allocation_check.expiry_check,
            source_allocation_check.expiry_status,
            source_allocation_check.dest_shop,
            source_allocation_check.dest_stock,
            source_allocation_check.dest_sales,
            source_allocation_check.dest_last_grn,
            source_allocation_check.dest_grn_age,
            source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_date,
            source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_plus_30,
            source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales,
            source_allocation_check.dest_cap AS dest_sales_used,
            source_allocation_check.priority_rank,
            source_allocation_check.recommended_qty,
            sum(source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY source_allocation_check.item_code, source_allocation_check.dest_shop ORDER BY source_allocation_check.priority_rank, source_allocation_check.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days, 999999), source_allocation_check.source_shop) AS cumulative_qty,
            (source_allocation_check.dest_cap - COALESCE(sum(source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) OVER (PARTITION BY source_allocation_check.item_code, source_allocation_check.dest_shop ORDER BY source_allocation_check.priority_rank, source_allocation_check.source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days, 999999), source_allocation_check.source_shop ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING), (0)::double precision)) AS dest_remaining_cap_before,
            ((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) AS dest_updated_stock,
                CASE
                    WHEN ((((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) > (0)::double precision) AND (GREATEST(source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales, (source_allocation_check.dest_sales)::double precision) > (0)::double precision)) THEN ceil(((((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) * (30.0)::double precision) / GREATEST(source_allocation_check.dest_wh_grn_30d_sales, (source_allocation_check.dest_sales)::double precision)))
                    WHEN (((source_allocation_check.dest_stock)::double precision + source_allocation_check.recommended_qty) > (0)::double precision) THEN (999)::double precision
                    ELSE (0)::double precision
                END AS dest_final_stock_days,
                CASE
                    WHEN (source_allocation_check.expiry_status = 'Expired'::text) THEN 'Item expired - cannot transfer'::text
                    WHEN ((source_allocation_check.expiry_status = 'Expiring'::text) AND (source_allocation_check.source_expiry_days < 30)) THEN 'Expiring soon - cannot transfer'::text
                    WHEN (source_allocation_check.cumulative_source_allocated >= (source_allocation_check.source_available)::double precision) THEN 'Source stock fully allocated'::text
                    WHEN ((source_allocation_check.recommended_qty = (0)::double precision) AND (source_allocation_check.uncapped_qty > (0)::double precision)) THEN 'Cap reached'::text
                    WHEN ((source_allocation_check.recommended_qty > (0)::double precision) AND (source_allocation_check.recommended_qty < source_allocation_check.uncapped_qty)) THEN 'Partial allocation'::text
                    WHEN (source_allocation_check.recommended_qty > (0)::double precision) THEN 'Full allocation'::text
                    ELSE 'No transfer needed'::text
                END AS remark
           FROM source_allocation_check
        )
 SELECT item_code,
    item_name,
    groups,
    sub_group,
    item_type,
    supplier_name,
    source_shop,
    source_stock,
    source_sales,
    source_last_grn,

    recommended_qty,
    cumulative_qty,
    dest_remaining_cap_before,
    dest_updated_stock,
    dest_final_stock_days,
    remark
   FROM final_results
  ORDER BY item_code, dest_shop, priority_rank, source_grn_age DESC, COALESCE(source_expiry_days, 999999), source_shop;

  # Materialized View Fixes - Item 157596 Overallocation Issue

## Issue Summary

**Problem**: Item 157596 was being over-allocated to LFS:
- LFS cap: 41 units
- **Actual allocation: 410 units** (10× overstock!)
- LFS would receive from 10 different sources, each giving 41 units

**Root Causes**:
1. ❌ Window function cumulative calculation was incorrect
2. ❌ Expiry threshold too lenient (< 0 instead of < 30)
3. ❌ Each destination not treated independently

---

## Critical Bugs Fixed

### Bug 1: Cumulative Allocation Per Destination

**BEFORE (BROKEN)**:
```sql
-- Window function calculated cumulative across ALL destinations
-- This caused each destination to get full cap allocation independently
SUM(CASE ... END) OVER (
    PARTITION BY item_code  -- ❌ WRONG: No dest_shop
    ORDER BY priority_rank, source_grn_age DESC
)
```

**AFTER (FIXED)**:
```sql
-- Window function now properly tracks per destination
SUM(CASE ... END) OVER (
    PARTITION BY item_code, dest_shop  -- ✅ CORRECT: Per destination
    ORDER BY priority_rank, source_grn_age DESC
)
```

**Impact**: Now LFS will get ONLY 41 units total from ALL sources combined, not 41 from each source.

---

### Bug 2: Expiry Threshold

**BEFORE (BROKEN)**:
```sql
-- Only blocked items with expiry < 0 (already expired)
-- Items with 1-29 days remaining were still transferred
WHEN source_expiry_days < 0 THEN 0  -- Too lenient
WHEN source_expiry_days BETWEEN 1 AND 30 THEN uncapped_qty  -- Still allocates!
```

**AFTER (FIXED)**:
```sql
-- Block items with < 30 days remaining shelf life
-- Only transfer items with >= 30 days expiry or no expiry tracking
WHEN source_expiry_days < 30 THEN 0  -- ✅ Blocks expiring items
WHEN source_expiry_days >= 30 THEN uncapped_qty  -- Safe to transfer
```

**Impact**: 
- Items with 82 days expiry: ✅ Now recommended
- Items with 1-29 days expiry: ❌ Blocked
- Items with negative expiry: ❌ Blocked

---

### Bug 3: Expiring Status Classification

**BEFORE**:
```sql
WHEN source_expiry_days BETWEEN 1 AND 30 THEN 'Expiring'
```

**AFTER**:
```sql
WHEN source_expiry_days BETWEEN 1 AND 29 THEN 'Expiring'
```

**Impact**: Items with exactly 30 days now classified as 'OK' (safe to transfer).

---

## Expected Results After Fix

### Item 157596 → LFS (Cap: 41 units)

**BEFORE FIX**:
| Source | Expiry Days | Recommended | Cumulative | Remark |
|--------|-------------|-------------|------------|---------|
| M04 | 82 | 41 | 41 | Cap reached ❌ |
| SD2 | 82 | 41 | 82 | Cap reached ❌ |
| TKD | 82 | 41 | 123 | Cap reached ❌ |
| BIB | 82 | 41 | 164 | Cap reached ❌ |
| ... | ... | 41 each | ... | **TOTAL: 410** ❌ |

**AFTER FIX**:
| Source | Expiry Days | Recommended | Cumulative | Remark |
|--------|-------------|-------------|------------|---------|
| M04 | 82 | 31 | 31 | Partial allocation ✅ |
| SD2 | 82 | 10 | 41 | Cap reached ✅ |
| TKD | 82 | 0 | 41 | Cap reached ✅ |
| BIB | 82 | 0 | 41 | Cap reached ✅ |
| ... | ... | 0 | 41 | **TOTAL: 41** ✅ |

---

## Allocation Logic (Corrected)

```
For each item → destination pair:
    1. Get destination cap (MAX of WH GRN +30d sales, regular 30d sales)
    2. Calculate gap: cap - current_stock
    3. Sort sources by:
       - Priority rank (if source is priority shop - shouldn't happen)
       - GRN age (DESC - oldest first, FIFO)
       - Expiry days (ASC - expiring first, FEFO)
    4. Allocate from sources until cumulative = cap:
       cumulative_before = SUM of previous allocations (this destination only)
       remaining = cap - cumulative_before
       allocate = MIN(source_available, remaining)
    5. Stop when cumulative reaches cap for THIS destination
```

**KEY**: `PARTITION BY item_code, dest_shop` ensures each destination has independent allocation tracking.

---

## Validation Queries

Run `test_fix_item_157596.sql` to verify:

1. **Test 1**: LFS recommendations should show valid allocations
2. **Test 2**: Cumulative never exceeds cap (41 for LFS)
3. **Test 3**: Expired items (< 0 days) show 0 recommended_qty
4. **Test 4**: Summary shows total_recommended ≤ cap for all destinations

---

## Remark Logic (Updated Priority)

```sql
CASE 
    -- Priority 1: Expiry issues (highest priority - safety)
    WHEN expiry_status = 'Expired' THEN 'Item expired - cannot transfer'
    WHEN expiry_status = 'Expiring' AND source_expiry_days < 30 THEN 'Expiring soon - cannot transfer'
    
    -- Priority 2: Source exhaustion
    WHEN cumulative_source_allocated >= source_available THEN 'Source stock fully allocated'
    
    -- Priority 3: Destination cap status (only if expiry OK)
    WHEN recommended_qty = 0 AND uncapped_qty > 0 THEN 'Cap reached'
    WHEN recommended_qty > 0 AND recommended_qty < uncapped_qty THEN 'Partial allocation'
    WHEN recommended_qty > 0 THEN 'Full allocation'
    
    ELSE 'No transfer needed'
END
```

**Key Change**: Expiry checks happen BEFORE cap checks, so you'll never see "Cap reached" for expired items.

   - Updated expiry classification from `1 AND 30` to `1 AND 29`
   - Added 'Expiring soon' remark for 1-29 day items

2. **test_fix_item_157596.sql** (NEW)

   - 4 tests covering different scenarios

## Technical Notes

**Window Function Partitioning**:
- `PARTITION BY item_code` = Cumulative across ALL destinations (WRONG)
- `PARTITION BY item_code, dest_shop` = Cumulative per destination (CORRECT)

**Expiry Safety**:
- Minimum 30 days shelf life required for transfer
- Expiring items (1-29 days) blocked from transfer
- Expired items (< 0 days) blocked from transfer

**FEFO Priority**:
- Expiring items (1-29 days) sorted first BUT blocked (0 allocation)
- OK items (≥ 30 days) sorted by expiry ASC (shorter expiry first among valid items)
- NULL expiry sorted last (treated as very long shelf life)
